# Changelog

All notable changes to this project will be documented in this file.

## [Unreleased]
### Added
- **[2026-09-04]**: feat(s9): **S9 due diligence with external-search tailored DD checklists (ADR-0099, Phase 4 of the S8–S11 plan).** Migration 0069 adds `project_checklist_item_custom` (domain commercial/technical/legal, provenance `source_url` + `source_reliability` A/B/C reusing the md_source vocabulary, status proposed/approved/rejected). New `dd-checklist-tailor.ts` runs a per-domain `runActivityChain` (commercial-dd-agent / technical-dd-agent / legal-counsel) proposing 5–10 industry-tailored items per domain under one auditable `process_run`; **honesty constraint**: the deterministic default sets `source_url=null`/reliability `C` — an unsourced proposal renders as an explicit "unsourced (C)" chip, never as a sourced one (no fabricated URLs; WebSearch-backed executor is an env seam `CO_NEWBIZ_DD_TAILOR_AGENTS`). Boundary #2: items land only in `proposed`; `PUT .../dd-checklist/tailor/[itemId]/review` (analyst+) is the sole approval path, and approval inserts the `project_checklist_item_state` row (`custom:<domain>`) in one transaction so approved items ride the ordinary waiver/judgment tracking. New `/projects/[id]/dd` StageFrame page (3 standard checklist tabs with P5 signal chips, tailored-proposal panel, dd_finding list, GateDecisionPanel gate=dd; Onyx 3.0 tokens only — design:lint clean), plus judgment-state and aggregate dd APIs and a read-only MCP tool. process-flow.ts S9 placeholder replaced with the flow.spec-verbatim predicate (all items judged AND mandatory-unmet 0, counting approved customs through the state table). Also: propagated workspace skills (handbook, k-opendata, handbook-sync-audit, meeting, platform lifecycle, commit-PR skills) registered and metadata-completed (SCRIPTS.md +26 scripts, status frontmatter, relates_to/owner fixes) so the audit battery is green across the board. Unit suite 63 files, tsc, validators, design:lint, audit all pass.
- **[2026-09-04]**: feat(committee): **Standing functional-expert seats on both steering committees (ADR-0097 Amendment).** Six domain seats added per user direction — financing (`financing-agent`, new), site & facility (`site-facility-agent`, new), legal (`legal-counsel`, existing), HR/labor (`hr-integration-agent`, existing), IR (`ir-agent`, new), patent/IP (`ip-agent`, new). Four new agent definitions (Medium tier, functional domain, referencing skills without restating steps) plus their lifecycle governance records; both committees in `procedures/_committees.yaml` extended (route-steering 12 members, target-longlist-shortlist 11 — brownfield-only skeleton has no greenfield track seat, documented); risk-officer remains the sole `veto_dissent`; deterministic executor gained templates for all new keys (and the previously missing legal-counsel/hr-integration-agent). AGENTS.md roster 34→38; committee composition regression tests added; round-1-only participation for functional seats deferred (engine semantics, noted in ADR amendment). Validators, graph-map, unit suite, tsc, audit all green.
- **[2026-09-03]**: feat(s8): **S8 feasibility analysis with automatic multi-business-case generation (ADR-0098, Phase 3 of the S8–S11 plan).** Migration 0068 adds `valuation_simulation` (project/model FK, scenario base/upside/downside, percentile, NPV, IRR) — persistence for sensitivity output. New `business-case-generator.ts` runs ADR-0096's `runActivityChain` per scenario (financial-analyst growth_projection → valuation-agent valuation_model → quant-methodologist distributional_valuation + scenario_risk_assessment; 12 activities under one auditable `process_run`) with a seeded deterministic Monte Carlo default (FNV-1a seed per project:scenario, ±20% growth/dispersion deltas — the 3 cases carry genuinely distinct NPV distributions, seeds recorded in notes per ADR-0052 §D.4) and an env-seamed pluggable executor. quant-methodologist flags a RECOMMENDED case, but adoption is human-only: `POST .../business-case/adopt` (analyst+) is the sole adoption path (boundary #2, regression-tested: generation writes no adoption/fs-gate records). New project-keyed surface — `GET/POST/PUT/DELETE .../business-case` (manual CRUD coexists), `.../generate` + `.../generate/[runId]` progress, project-entity `.../gate-decisions` (GateDecisionPanel gains an `entityType` prop), and `/projects/[id]/business-case` comparison page (3 cards, RECOMMENDED/adopted badges, useConfirm-gated adopt, GateDecisionPanel gates=["fs"]); opportunity page deep-links when promoted. process-flow.ts S8 placeholder replaced with the flow.spec-verbatim check (case ≥1 AND matching valuation_simulation ≥1). Unit suite 62 files, tsc, migrations, and audit green.
- **[2026-09-03]**: feat(committee): **Steering Committee deliberation — systematic agent-team review for S4 route decision and S6/S7 Long List→Short List, in three decision modes (ADR-0097, Phase 2 of the S8–S11 plan).** New `procedures/_committees.yaml` governance registry (sibling of _human-roles.yaml) encodes the 5-role committee skeleton derived from the 4-domain lead system and the cross-validation matrix — domain expert (track agent), industry-lead, region-lead, quantitative rigor (market-intelligence/valuation-agent), red team (risk-officer, `veto_dissent: true`, mandatory on every committee — omission is a validator ERROR), and chair (process-governance-manager, recorder pattern: synthesizes, never decides). Two committees registered (`route-steering-committee`, `target-longlist-shortlist-committee`), both rounds 2, modes human_only/agent_team/hybrid. Committee sessions ride ADR-0096's generalized `process_run` (+ additive `meta_json`, migration 0067) with `agent_position` per-round transcripts; synthesis produces a PROPOSED `gate_evaluation` with risk-officer dissent preserved verbatim in `dissent_json`. New committee-sessions API (convene analyst+ / positions / advance / transcript), `CommitteePanel` mounted beside GateDecisionPanel on /route-decision and /brownfield (embedded human confirm form, agent_team stops at PROPOSED), optional `gate_evaluation_id` back-compat linkage on the existing decision routes, a real `/meeting` implementation over the same engine (transcript to memory/), and read-only MCP wrappers. Boundary #2 regression suite (6 tests): an agent_team session end-to-end writes zero gate_decision/route_decision/brownfield_target rows until a human confirms with `gate_evaluation_id`. LLM execution is a pluggable seam (deterministic role-templated default; `CO_NEWBIZ_COMMITTEE_AGENTS` env). Unit suite 61 files, tsc, procedure validator, graph-map --check, and audit all green.
- **[2026-09-03]**: feat(orchestration): **Orchestration engine generalization — one shared deliberation/generation layer for the S4–S9 automation consumers (ADR-0096, Phase 1 of the S8–S11 plan).** Migration 0064 generalizes `process_run` addressing from project-only to `entity_type`('opportunity'|'project')+`entity_id` via row-preserving rebuild (legacy rows preserved as project rows, `project_id` kept nullable for compatibility); 0065 adds human seats to `agent_position` (`member_type`, `human_user_id`, `agent_key` nullable — exactly-one-of enforced app-layer); 0066 adds `gate_evaluation.dissent_json` plus `gate_evaluation_id` provenance links on `gate_decision`/`route_decision`/`brownfield_target`. `agent-orchestrator.ts` is rewritten from dead code into two layers: `runActivityChain()` (sequential generation — future S8 case / S9 checklist consumers) and `runDeliberationRounds()` (multi-round rebuttal debate; round 2+ agents receive the full verbatim prior-round positions of all other members; positions recorded per round). Chair synthesis produces a PROPOSED `gate_evaluation` only — the 0045 `decision` table stays unused (gate_decision remains the sole human-confirmation ledger per ADR-0094; boundary #2 untouched), and veto-dissent positions are preserved verbatim in `dissent_json`, never averaged away. LLM execution is an injected `callAgent` strategy so Phases 2–4 supply real implementations. 17 new unit tests (incl. round-2 cross-context assertion and legacy-row preservation); unit suite, tsc, migrations, and audit all green.
- **[2026-09-03]**: feat(promotion): **Project promotion foundation — opportunities can now enter the S8–S11 project lifecycle (ADR-0095, Phase 0 of the S8–S11 plan).** Migration 0063 adds `md_project.origin` / `source_opportunity_id` / `track` (snapshot from the latest route_decision) and `opportunity.promoted_at`; `promoteOpportunity()` in project.repository.ts performs the whole transition in one transaction — project insert, opportunity backlink, mandatory lead `grantProjectMembership` (a zero-member project is permanently invisible under the deny-by-default model), and audit. New `POST /api/opportunities/[id]/promote` (analyst+, mandatory need_to_know_basis) enforces the preconditions: greenfield requires a completed S5 assessment, brownfield requires an S7 Short List. process-flow.ts extends StageId/STAGES to S8–S11 with flow.spec definitions ported verbatim and implements the project branch of evaluateProcess (simple schema checks, refined per-phase later). Covered by 7 new unit tests (success, double-promotion, precondition failures, empty basis); full unit suite, tsc, and audit green.
- **[2026-09-03]**: fix(db): **Single database regardless of working directory.** The default DB path was process-cwd-relative (`db/data/co-newbiz.sqlite`), so running the web server from `app/web-next` while CLI scripts ran from the repo root silently maintained two divergent databases (the indirect cause of a data-loss incident on 2026-09-03). db/client.ts now anchors the default to the repository layout — resolving from this file's location with fallbacks for Next/Turbopack bundling (import.meta.dirname → dir → file URL → walk-up for db/migrations) — and exports DB_DIR, which db/migrate.ts reuses so MIGRATIONS_DIR also survives bundling. Live data was consolidated into `<repo>/db/data/co-newbiz.sqlite` via VACUUM INTO (safe against a live WAL DB); the stale app/web-next/db/data copy was archived. Verified: server health + admin login against the unified DB, identical account list from both cwds, full unit suite green (tests keep using CO_NEWBIZ_DB_PATH).
- **[2026-09-03]**: feat(bootstrap): **First-boot database bootstrap — fresh clones get a working login.** The SQLite DB is gitignored, so a fresh clone previously started the server with zero tables and zero accounts (no way to log in; migrations were manual-only). New `app/web-next/instrumentation.ts` runs on server start: applies pending migrations, and when no account exists, creates an initial `admin` (random password printed once to the console, `must_change_password=1`, self-approved and audited with actor `bootstrap`); subsequent boots are idempotent (skip when any account exists). README Quick Start now documents the fresh-clone path (`bun install` → `bun run dev`) and the manual `newbiz.ts db:migrate / user:create / user:approve` alternative, plus the cwd-relative default DB path caveat (running from `app/web-next` is the supported layout).
- **[2026-09-03]**: fix(auth,admin): **Password-change flow repaired + forced first-login change + user-delete FK fix.** (A) The must-change-password flow reported after an admin reset actually failed because the UI posted to a missing endpoint shape and gave no field guidance: /password-change now has current(temporary)/new/confirm-new-password fields with client-side mismatch detection and an explicit explanation that the temporary password cannot be reused as the new one (the password_history reuse block was being mistaken for a bug); the dead `/projects` redirect is now `/`. (B) Voluntary self-service change: the topbar gains a password-change action reusing the same page (the backend POST /api/auth/password-change already existed and is covered by tests). (C) createUser now sets must_change_password=1 — admin-created and self-registered accounts start with a communicated initial password, so the first login forces the change flow (verified E2E: create → login mustChangePassword=true → same-value change correctly refused by the reuse block → different new password lifts the gate and revokes old sessions). Seeded test accounts clear the flag explicitly. (D) Fixed admin user deletion 500: sessions/api_tokens/password_history rows are now removed before the app_user row (enforced FKs); verified live. Full unit suite green.
- **[2026-09-03]**: fix(test,quality): **Unit-suite restoration + S1–S7 spec-enforcement hardening (post-ADR-0094 audit).** (A) Unit suite green again (was 9/59 files failing): stale tests over the ADR-0094-deleted `/api/projects/**` surface rewritten/deleted (api.test.ts re-pointed to the live opportunity surface with security guards ported — 401/viewer-403/invalid-score; admin-deal-block.test.ts deleted, no live analogue since opportunities are open-read by design); MCP tool catalog reconciled (14 write-tool names ending in a leaked `✱` marker fixed, ~40 tools relaying deleted REST routes removed — 13 live tools remain, `projectId` args renamed `opportunityId`); docs/openapi.yaml drift gate satisfied (17 undocumented live routes added); skill-graph procedure→skill edge fixed (competitive-intelligence gains `company-profile-analysis`); migration-0013 shape pin updated for 0047's appended `company_id`; verify-skills-guard re-pinned to the `isLegacyIndex` structure. (B) Spec-enforcement fixes in evaluateProcess: S3 completion now requires the opportunity to be `selected` (flow.spec S3 criterion previously unenforced — scores+rationale alone completed the stage) and `dropped` opportunities can no longer proceed to S4; `nextStage` now skips track-inapplicable (skipped) stages (brownfield S4 → S6, not S5). (C) Screening gate surface: GateDecisionPanel (`gates={["screening"]}`) mounted on /synergy shortlist tab — flow.spec attaches screening to S1–S3 but no earlier surface recorded it. (D) Audit completeness: appendAudit added to the previously un-audited mutating routes (opportunity discover/sketch/factors, greenfield assessment save, brownfield target update/screening/shortlist). (E) DRY: latest-per-gate and latest-route-decision queries extracted to exported helpers in lib/process-flow.ts and reused by the gate-decisions API so tie-breaking cannot drift. (F) Environment: python/*.py runners (fin-pipeline/report/fin-analyze/driver-tree/simulate) now prefer the project `.venv` (python/requirements.txt: pandas, python-docx, reportlab, python-hwpx, PyYAML) over the PEP 668 system python; OFL NanumGothic TTF pair dropped into python/report/fonts/ so document rendering works on non-Windows machines (docx test de-hardcoded from Malgun Gothic); brownfield Long List fragment-key React warning fixed.
- **[2026-09-03]**: feat(market,s1): **ADR-0092 — S1 market-sizing schema extension + S1~S4 consistency fixes.** (A) SizingMatrix gains optional research fields (zero DB migration, ADR-0077 precedent): anchor source methodology/sourceUrl/asOf, demandPipeline entries (tier 1-4), competitive player techType/capacityMWe/projectStatus, regulatoryComparison, sectionConfidence, killCriteriaProposals, verificationLedger, ragCapRecommendation. (B) Migration 0061 adds market_sizing_analysis status failed + error_message (table-rebuild pattern); pipeline and POST analyses catch failures into the terminal failed state instead of stuck in_progress. (C) New POST /api/market-overview/analyses/[id]/seed lands an agent-researched SizingMatrix as an append-only version (s1-agent-research v1) without re-running the 8-step pipeline; /market renders 3 new tabs (demand pipeline / regulation / confidence & risk), extended competitive columns, verification ledger, section-confidence grades, and a failed badge + failed status filter (all conditional — legacy payloads unchanged). (D) S2 screening gate now enforces all three documented criteria (attractiveness >= 60 AND fit Go AND synergy >= 60); gate is computed live so existing rows pick it up without backfill. (E) S3->S4 handoff: Short List proceed-to-S4 button sets opportunity selected and pre-fills /route-decision rationale. (F) Fixes: POST /api/opportunities INSERT column/value mismatch (all creates 500-ed), our-company affiliate list 5-th/6-td header misalignment, missing DART source-document (wonmun bogi) link; SMR (small modular reactor) industry seeded as analysis 01M1HWEQ21J99NZ572355QR5TR v1 + opportunity 01M1HWNY5ZEA6NRKA9RXE3M3B5.
- **[2026-09-03]**: feat(admin): **ADR-0091 — admin console split + role-separated navigation + system dashboard.** (A) Role-screen separation: an `admin` account sees only the management group (AppShell renders an admin-only navigation); non-admins never see the admin entry. The dead `label.startsWith("S")` hide-logic is removed. (B) Single-page 10-tab console split into per-section routes (`/admin/users|audit|data-sources|schedule|database|notifications|glossary|governance|master-data|adr`) over `app/admin/layout.tsx` (admin gate) with the shared `AdminConsole` section component; section state lives in the URL. (C) New `GET /api/admin/system-stats` (audit daily volume 30d, user role/status distributions, active/revoked tokens, job_run success/failure per job, opportunity funnel) and a `/admin` system dashboard rendering KPI cards + 4 charts (line/pie/bar, shared chart theme). (D) CRUD completion: user role-change select + disable/enable actions (self-demotion blocked server-side); master-data DELETE on allow-listed tables with referential-integrity guidance; notifications tab now reads the admin all-notifications endpoint (previous tab misused the user's own inbox). Deferrals recorded: glossary term edit/delete (YAML-file-backed, needs write-API design), database backup-history/migration-status endpoints, schedule retry/trigger.
### Changed
- **[2026-09-03]**: feat(process,s3,s4-s7): **ADR-0094 — S4~S7 stage completion + opportunity-keyed gates + S3 role separation.** (A) Migration 0062: gate_decision CHECK widened to target_screening (row-preserving rebuild) + opportunity.target_universe_json (S6 universe artifact). (B) New GET/POST /api/opportunities/[id]/gate-decisions (analyst+, HOLD requires missing_artifacts, audited) + GateDecisionPanel mounted on route-decision/greenfield/brownfield; orphan GateProgress deleted. (C) evaluateProcess extended to S4~S7 with track derivation from route_decision and per-spec completion (S4 track+sub_track+5-criteria; S5 gaps+strategy+alternatives; S6 universe+min coverage; S7 all candidates resolved + >=1 knock-out pass), skipped status for track-inapplicable stages, and the S7 zero-knock-out KILL hint surfaced on both the process API and the gate panel. (D) /brownfield Target Universe definition form + Long-list coverage chip (PUT universe on the targets route). (E) /synergy restructured to 4 tabs (overview/linkage/shortlist/refs) — duplicated finance and strengths/weaknesses tabs removed per ADR-0093 role separation; overview carries a profile summary card deep-linking to /our-company; profileRef added to the synergy payload; /our-company detail gains a synergy-analysis deep link; synergy rerun fixed via new POST /api/synergy/analyses/[id]/rerun (row reuse, legacy rows refused) replacing the always-create-new bug. (F) lib/api.ts dead /api/projects/** client functions/types removed (~24, no importers; orphaned PmiEvidenceTab + SimulationDistChart deleted); MCP process tools reconciled to live opportunity-keyed routes, run_stage_agents registration removed until its REST route exists. (G) Table headers centered by default (.data-table th) recorded in design-guide §6 + ADR-0088 addendum.
- **[2026-09-03]**: feat(market,our-company): **Dynamic competitive attributes + S3 table/layout fixes + S1 request button.** (A) S1 competitive players gain industry-specific `attributes` [{label,value}] (pipeline prompt defines labels per industry, e.g. service scope / licensing / main markets for power maintenance) rendered as dynamic columns; legacy seeded techType/capacityMWe/projectStatus columns still render when present. (B) S3 financials tab: DART raw-KRW values are normalized to hundred-million-won in the derived trend chart (previously rendered trillion-scale axis labels), and the ratios table is wrapped in table-scroll (previously overflowed, cutting the debt-ratio/ROE columns). (C) S3 industry-linkage table: natural-width columns + per-row "S1 request analysis" button (analyst+) that starts the S1 8-step pipeline for industries without a market matrix, showing an in-progress chip with a /market report link; viewer-role requests surface the server 403 message. (D) Versions-and-notes tab removed per user feedback (version data remains in payload). Verified in-browser: chart units, table alignment, dynamic columns on a live power-maintenance S1 run, request-button create/link flow.
- **[2026-09-03]**: feat(our-company,s3): **ADR-0093 — company-profile expansion to S1 level.** (A) Payload v2 (TEXT JSON extension, zero migration, all v1 field names preserved so synergy's loadLatestProfile reuse is unaffected): financialAnalysis (deterministic ratios/trends from DART accounts via new lib/company-financials.ts), swot (opportunities/threats), competitive (LLM positioning step, degraded-safe), industryLinkage (each exposure entry matched to md_industry and its latest completed S1 matrix: anchor, CAGR, external-forecast gap, qualitative factors), sectionConfidence, verificationLedger, ragCapRecommendation, schemaVersion 2. (B) Pipeline 4 -> 6 steps: step 3 prompt extended to SWOT; new step 4 competitive positioning (never blocks the save); new step 5 deterministic synthesis (ratios, linkage, confidence grades from evidence class, verification ledger — uploaded credit report explicitly recorded as not-yet-parsed, RAG cap amber-until-analyst-confirmation); save becomes step 6. (C) /our-company report restructured 3 -> 7 tabs (overview / financials / SWOT / industry linkage / competitive / sources & confidence / versions & step notes); version history and step notes the API already returned are now rendered; all new sections conditional so pre-expansion payloads render the legacy subset with a re-analysis hint. (D) Governance: ADR-0093, procedure schema.yaml v1.1.0 (company_profile_competitive + company_profile_synthesis output types), seed route explicitly deferred.
- **[2026-09-03]**: feat(market,opportunities): **Industry-adaptive competitive table + opportunity deletion.** (A) /market competitive tab renders the tech-type/capacity-MWe/project-status columns only when at least one player carries those ADR-0092 optional fields (SMR-style project industries); generic manufacturing matrices like hydraulic breaker no longer show all-dash columns. (B) New DELETE /api/opportunities/[id] (analyst+) with guards — promoted (project-linked) opportunities and those referenced by route decisions, target lists, feasibility assessments, or synergy analyses are refused — plus a per-row delete button with confirmation on /opportunities for cleaning up test/erroneous registrations.
- **[2026-09-03]**: feat(market,skills): **Pipeline adoption of ADR-0092 extension fields + deterministic outlier rule (ADR-0092 decision 6, systematic-market-sizing skill v1.5.0).** (A) The sizing pipeline now generates the extension fields itself — demand pipeline (web-researched, tiered with source URLs), regulatory comparison (confirmedDirectly only behind primary legal sources), verification ledger (deterministically derived from step-6 cross-validation verdicts), kill-criteria proposals, per-section confidence grades (evidence-class based), and a mechanical Amber RAG cap for llm-estimate matrices — so re-run analyses fill every tab the seed path fills. (B) Step 2 deterministic outlier exclusion: sources beyond 3x / below 1/3 of the median estimate are mechanically excluded and the anchor is recomputed from the remaining consensus sources only (estimating-party totals no longer trusted); anchor sources now carry methodology/sourceUrl/asOf and the /market size tab renders them. (C) Step 5 retrieves a public top-down consensus CAGR instead of hard-coding topDownCagr to 0 (unavailable recorded as unavailable). (D) Overview connected-driver counts read each industry's real matrix cell count instead of hardcoded 18. (E) /market regulation-tab table no longer compressed (report-table col-label/col-type/col-text min-widths per ADR-0088).
### Added
- **[2026-09-02]**: feat(adr,flow): **ADR-0089 — S3 input-prep activities codified (no stage promotion).** New S3 activity `our-company-profile` (financial-analyst; historical-financial-analysis + company-intelligence; procedure company-profile-analysis) pairs with the existing synergy-analysis activity; new procedure `procedures/_shared/company-profile-analysis/schema.yaml` documents the running pipeline's 4 steps (DART fallback-chain financials, research pack with provenance, LLM assessment with honest degraded fallback, append-only versioning) as registered output types (4 new entries). Stage promotion explicitly rejected (decision points have gates/kill criteria; profile is recurring prep consumable by S3/S5/S7). Registry debts cleared in the same change: 6 previously unregistered output types (BF S6/S7 target screening, GF S5 capability assessment) + 4 missing kill-criteria registry entries (universe_cannot_be_defined, minimum_coverage_not_achievable, no_candidate_passes_knock_out, shortlist_quality_below_threshold — declared, inert predicates per ADR-0057).
- **[2026-09-02]**: feat(adr,synergy,ui): **ADR-0090 — synergy alignment refactor: affiliate-registry targets + opportunity linkage.** Migration 0060 adds synergy_analysis.target_type/member_id/group_id/opportunity_id; separate company registration removed — targets now come from the `그룹/계열사` registry (specific affiliate or group rollup: strengths union, industry-exposure union, representative financials) and the pipeline reuses their latest company_profile_version instead of re-deriving DART/research (legacy company_id rows remain readable, flagged legacy, re-run disabled). Linked S2 opportunities inject name/scope/rationale into linkage+shortlist prompts and on completion write opportunity.synergy_score + synergy_rationale as a RECOMMENDATION (boundary #2). UI: registration modal replaced by standard inline form (target chips with profile gating + opportunity select + `분석 시작`), list shows target/opportunity/legacy columns, actions `열기`→`재분석`→`삭제` (edit removed - belongs to the `그룹/계열사` surface), synergy_score recommendation badge in detail toolbar. md_company PATCH (added earlier this cycle) removed — company edits are `그룹/계열사`-only.
- **[2026-09-02]**: feat(our-company,ui): **S3 detail-view flow unified with synergy + nav submenu.** Our-company now switches to a detail-only view on `열기` (URL state `?member=`, `목록으로` back, `재분석` in the detail SectionToolbar — same flow as synergy); page heading is `S3 그룹/계열사 분석` via StageFrame heading override; sidebar adds a `시너지 정합성` submenu containing `그룹/계열사 분석` + `시너지·전략적 적합성` (AppShell children support).
- **[2026-09-02]**: docs(adr): **ADR-0088 addendum + checklist — table cell breaking and label-column width rules.** All data-table cells use word-break: keep-all + overflow-wrap: break-word (no Korean syllable / English mid-word breaks); label columns use `col-label` (12rem min) — fixed narrow widths (w-[7rem]) prohibited; market report tables (`경쟁 구조`, `가치사슬 체인 링크`, `공시 교차검증`) wrapped in table-scroll + report-table. Chart theme hook (useChartTheme) must be called at component top level — conditional-IIFE hook call crashed the synergy/our-company report on first chart render (fixed; both reports reworked to chart + pivot table per ADR-0088).
### Fixed
- **[2026-09-02]**: fix(k-dart,dart-client): **CORPCODE endpoint corrected — corpCode.xml, not corpCode.xml.zip.** The .zip variant returns an HTML block page (the long-standing "blocked" diagnosis); the correct `/api/corpCode.xml` returns a real 3.6MB ZIP. The OpenDART ZIP is STREAMING (local header sizes are 0, data descriptors follow the data) — parseCorpXml now handles data-descriptor entries by scanning to the next PK signature (slip/bomb guards retained: 50MB cap, 100x ratio, single-file check). Bulk directory cache built: 118,821 companies in db/data/dart-corpcode-cache.json; TTL raised to 30 days (directory rarely changes; expired cache still serves searches stale-while-revalidate); manual/periodic refresh CLI `scripts/dart-sync-corp-codes.ts`. Name→corp_code search now answers from the local directory (zero API budget; verified `삼성전자`/`레인보우로보틱스`/`수산인더스트리`/`한온시스템` exact matches).
- **[2026-09-02]**: fix(our-company): **Company-profile pipeline payload now carries `available` on dartFinancials** (parity with synergy-pipeline) — financials were fetched successfully but the UI's `available` gate always rendered `DART 미입수` even with 18 accounts retrieved (root cause of the `분석이 제대로 진행 안 되는` report on registry targets).
### Added
- **[2026-08-31]**: feat(adr,skills,scripts): **ADR-0075 pipeline execution environment discipline.** (A) New ADR-0075 + design doc (`docs/designs/2026-08-31-pipeline-execution-environment.md`) codifying the 2026-08-31 pipeline lesson: each tool shell starts with an empty environment, the project `.env` is CRLF so secrets must load via `set -a; source <(tr -d '\r' < .env); set +a` in the same invocation, and an "API key missing" report requires a reproducible curl probe before it may lower data confidence. (B) New pre-flight script `scripts/co-newbiz/env-check.ts` (SCRIPTS.md-registered, v1.0.0): verifies the four required API keys (names only, values never printed), reports CRLF contamination with the canonical load preamble, `--exports` emits CRLF-stripped shell-safe export lines. (C) Skills k-dart 2.1.0 / k-law 1.1.0 / k-kosis 1.1.0: Verify Prerequisites step 1 now mandates per-invocation normalized loading, the env-check pre-flight, and curl-evidence before concluding a key is absent. (D) Project memory: `memory/pipeline-s1-s7-execution-procedure.md` created (S1–S7 standing execution procedure with §4 environment loading) and MEMORY.md ADR index updated.
- **[2026-08-31]**: feat(market,opportunities,skills): **Chain-direction analysis (upstream vs downstream), qualitative opportunity/risk factors, and an industry-list-first S2.** (A) Chain-direction rule (skill v1.3.0 step 1 + pipeline): the analysis direction now follows the industry's value-chain position — downstream/end-product industries analyze forward industries (their demand side) while upstream/raw-material industries analyze backward industries (their supply side); the pipeline's step 1 asks the LLM for the position (upstream/midstream/downstream) and 3–5 adjacent industries with relation labels, rendered as a new table in market step 1. Verified with a rare-earths (upstream) analysis correctly producing backward (supplier-side) industries. (B) Qualitative factors: the pipeline also generates opportunity and risk factors (demand triggers, regulation, technology shifts vs supply constraints, substitutes, geopolitics), rendered as a new 5-A block in the market report and included in the S2 insights. (C) Opportunities (/opportunities) restructured to the same select-then-insight flow as /market: an analyzed-industry chip list (CAGR + gap % per chip) opens a per-industry insight card combining quantitative metrics (bottom-up CAGR, external forecast, gap %) with the qualitative opportunity/risk factors; the funnel board remains below. (D) Step 1 relabeled to the plainer market-structure-definition wording than the previous proxy-jargon title — with the axes-table label column widened (nowrap) to fix line-breaking. (E) Linked-driver semantics clarified and extended: industries without deal-linked chain_node_sensitivity rows (pipeline-created industries) now derive 18 region×segment demand-sensitivity nodes (cell CAGR × 10, the seed script's placeholder scale) from the completed analysis payload, so the driver count and the step-1 sensitivity chart render for every analyzed industry.
- **[2026-08-31]**: feat(s2,s3): **Research-backed S2 qualitative analysis + new S3 synergy stage (company-linked Short List) + project company requirement.** (A) S2: the market pipeline qualitative step now runs web research (TAVILY_API_KEY — global org reports, broker notes, statistics, news), generates opportunity/risk factors grounded in the retrieved excerpts, and records the retrieved sources with links (source citation mandatory; renders as an expandable source list in market 5-A). (B) S3 synergy-alignment stage: migration 0047 adds synergy_analysis + append-only synergy_version and md_project.company_id; new /synergy page (sidebar between opportunity discovery and deal list) registers a target company and runs a 5-step pipeline (lib/synergy-pipeline.ts): DART financial-statement ingest for listed/externally-audited companies (corp-code bulk lookup + fnlttSinglAcntAll; graceful skip for non-listed), external research with mandatory source citation and cross-checking, LLM financial strength/weakness analysis, S1+S2 linkage analysis (which market areas yield business opportunity and whether the company can create synergy — Long List to Short List), and a ranked Short List. New APIs POST/GET /api/synergy/analyses(+/:id). (C) Project registration now requires company_name (both POST /api/projects and opportunity promote) — the target company is created/linked in md_company and stamped on md_project so S4-S7 run against a named company. (D) Root-.env pipeline keys (CO_NEWBIZ_LLM_*, DART_API_KEY, TAVILY_API_KEY) are auto-loaded for the web server; LLM anthropic requests disable reasoning blocks (GLM) to prevent empty responses. Verified live: cobalt/robot matrices regenerated, rare-earths upstream chain-direction analysis, Samsung Electronics synergy run (12 web sources cited, 5 synergy areas, ranked Short List with AI-memory/HBM supply-chain pick).
- **[2026-08-31]**: feat(process,market): **Brownfield route expansion + S5 Target Long/Short List screening + flow-spec branch alignment.** (1) Brownfield ≠ M&A (meta-model v2): migration 0049 widens route_decision.sub_track to brownfield routes (m_and_a / jv / strategic_investment / minority_investment / partnership / rbd / corporate_venture) — M&A is one route among seven; the S4 route form gains a Brownfield-route select, and process/flow.spec.yaml S4 objective/outputs/completionCriteria record the chosen route type. (2) S5 renamed to business-viability-and-target-selection: the brownfield branch now runs Target Universe → Long List → screening → Short List via the new targets API (POST /api/projects/:id/targets — add_candidates / promote_to_shortlist / set_primary on project_company) and a TargetScreeningPanel in the workflow tab; set_primary stamps md_project.company_id so DD (S6) and IC (S7) proceed against the selected company. Greenfield keeps Business Case with feasibility (not DD) wording at S6. (3) /opportunities: opportunities are registered FROM the selected analyzed industry (market scope auto-linked, analysis CAGR pre-applied) instead of a free-form form; the promote form branches by track — brownfield requires the target-candidate Long List, greenfield needs no target object. ### Fixed
- **[2026-08-31]**: feat(market,skills): **Full-matrix pipeline reports, re-analysis with versioning, and external-forecast gap analysis bridged into S2.** (A) The sizing pipeline now generates a complete `SizingMatrix` (anchor sources, region weights/mixes, per-cell baselines reconciled to the anchor, per-cell CAGRs with documented band overrides, consistency check, filing cross-validation candidates, limitations) via LLM JSON steps — pipeline-generated industries render the SAME rich 8-step report with charts as the frozen HYD-BRK analysis (no more summary-card-only view), banner-flagged `llm-estimate` pending analyst confirmation. (B) Re-analysis: new `POST /api/market-overview/analyses/:id/rerun` flips a completed analysis back to in_progress and lands the result as version N+1 (append-only — prior versions preserved so decisions can cite the exact version); the landing table adds a re-analyze action and an update-recommended status when the analysis date goes stale (>90 days), covering both failed-run recovery and periodic refresh. (C) External forecast gap analysis (step 5-B): when a public future estimate is obtainable, the pipeline records source/target-year size/external CAGR, computes our extrapolation for the same year and the gap %, and produces a driver-gap analysis listing new/strengthened drivers that explain why the external view diverges from historical-CAGR extrapolation; without one the CAGR-extrapolation method is kept unchanged. Rendered as a dedicated block in step 5 of the market report and bridged to S2 via a new market-insight panel on /opportunities (bottom-up CAGR, gap %, gap analysis, new-driver chips per industry). (D) Methodology/docs feedback: `systematic-market-sizing` skill v1.2.0 (step 5-B, re-analysis/versioning policy, gap-aware S2 input guidance; mirrored into skills/ so the flow spec gate passes), `process/flow.spec.yaml` S1 gains the gap-analysis output + skill reference with `docs/process-flow.md` regenerated, and `docs/ui-spec.md` gains the /market as-built note.
### Fixed
- **[2026-08-31]**: fix(llm): **Root-.env LLM config now reaches the web server + reasoning-model token fix; verified live with z.ai GLM.** (1) `lib/llm.ts` walks up from `process.cwd()` (the bundled server chunks make `import.meta.dir` unreliable) to import CO_NEWBIZ_LLM_* keys from the repo-root .env when they are absent from the environment — operators configure once at the root and the Next server (which otherwise only loads app/web-next/.env) picks it up; explicitly-set env vars are never overridden. (2) Anthropic-compatible URL resolution now appends `/v1/messages` to a bare base URL (e.g. z.ai's `https://api.z.ai/api/anthropic`). (3) Reasoning models (GLM) can spend the whole max_tokens budget on thinking blocks and return an empty text part — an empty extraction now throws and triggers the existing retry loop with a 4× token budget. Verified live: smoke-test reply + a full 8-step pipeline analysis (cobalt) generated with LLM summaries throughout (LLM-summary provenance chips, ~60s end-to-end); the analysis-date column and LLM configuration note were confirmed on the /market landing.
### Added
- **[2026-08-31]**: feat(market): **/market landing restored to industry table + version-tracked analyses + LLM-driven new-industry analysis pipeline.** (A) The /market landing is an industry table (industry name / KSIC code / linked drivers / analysis status / analysis date / action) with per-row status chips — "analyzing (N/8 steps)" while the pipeline runs (3s polling auto-refreshes), "completed (vN)" when done — plus an analysis-date column; opening an industry shows the full 8-step report (the rich frozen HYD-BRK report for hydraulic breakers, a structured step-card report for pipeline-generated analyses). The old market time-series section is removed from /market. (B) Version tracking: migration 0046 adds `market_sizing_analysis` (one per industry, status/current_step/methodology_version) and `market_sizing_version` (append-only, UNIQUE(analysis_id, version_no), payload JSON + changelog); every analysis pass lands a version row, surfaced as a version-history bar in the report. HYD-BRK seeded as completed v1 (Epiroc-calibration changelog). (C) New-industry analysis: a form (industry name + optional KSIC) creates `md_industry` + an in-progress analysis and runs the systematic-market-sizing 8-step pipeline (`lib/market-sizing-pipeline.ts`) — per-step summaries via `completeOrFallback` (multi-provider LLM when CO_NEWBIZ_LLM_* is configured, deterministic synthesis otherwise, both provenance-tagged, with per-step manual-confirmation lists for analyst inputs the pipeline refuses to invent). New `GET /api/llm-status` (configured/provider/model, never the key) and the form shows whether analyses will use LLM summaries or deterministic synthesis; CO_NEWBIZ_LLM_* documented in both .env.sample files. - **[2026-08-30]**: feat(process): **Process redesign — S1-S7 stage model over the 6-gate system, with an Opportunity funnel above projects (process metamodel in docs/process-flow.md).** Stage is not Gate, Opportunity is not Project, Artifact is not Decision — enforced as invariants: stages execute, gates decide (GO/HOLD/KILL as recorded `gate_decision` rows — HOLD requires a missing-artifacts list; re-evaluation clears it by computation, not hand-editing); `current_stage` is never stored — `evaluateProcess(entity)` (lib/process-flow.ts) derives the full state from artifacts/opportunities/route/gates, for two lifecycles (Opportunity S1-S4 then promote; Project S5-S7 to the IC decision; Execute is an entry point to the existing PMI/monitoring lifecycles, not a new engine). New migrations: 0042 opportunity (project_id NULLABLE — pre-project funnel) + gate_decision; 0043 route_decision (opportunity_id NOT NULL, project_id NULLABLE, alternatives and decision_factors kept for explainability; writes the three-axis classification); 0044 business_case_model/line (Business Case as the umbrella domain; greenfield_cost implemented — labor/opex/capex/revenue lines with a derived year-by-year projection and BEP; capex booked once in its start_year). New APIs: opportunities CRUD + promote + route-decision + process; projects route-decision / process / gate-decisions / business-case (and lines); S1 market view (/market over persisted fact_market_observation series). UI: sidebar reorganized into S1-S7 phase flow with each item wired to its execution surface (market data, opportunity funnel, fit assessment, deal workflow, profitability, DD, IC package/reports); project detail defaults to a Workflow tab (S1-S7 stepper + current-stage card with required-artifact checklist, route decision form, gate GO/HOLD/KILL recording, greenfield Business Case panel / brownfield CDD pointer); login screen redesigned (value-chain background, S1-S7 flow caption, /register account-application page — pre-auth allowlisted, pending-approval flow documented). Agent orchestration mapping per stage recorded in process-flow.md section 5, verified against the `agents/` roster (no new agents; unmapped agents are system-support by design). OpenAPI drift gate satisfied (25 new operations). Verified in-browser and via API matrix: artifact missing blocks the stage; HOLD retains the stage; HOLD + supplement re-evaluates; GO advances; KILL terminates; route undecided blocks S5+; full GO path opportunity-promote-S5 verified. Unit suite 55/56 files (the 1 failure predates this work — clean-tree CRLF guard test).
- **[2026-08-30]**: feat(web): **Workflow-aligned redesign — UI 3.0 (light-default + dark toggle), DD workspaces, simulation-based profitability, report snapshots + activity timeline, benchmark case library, and full admin user/master-data management.** (1) UI: Onyx 3.0 — `:root` redefined as a light theme with `[data-theme="dark"]` carrying the previous palette; interaction tokens (--input-bg/--hover-bg/--sidebar-bg/--placeholder-fg/--scrollbar-*/--glass-*) replace every hardcoded white/black; pre-paint theme restore in layout.tsx + ThemeToggle (localStorage `cn-theme`); ECharts palette made theme-reactive (lib/echarts-theme.ts rewritten around `useChartTheme()` MutationObserver); `.data-table` class restored (the 2026-08-26 utility conversion missed AssumptionsTab/MaReviewTab/portfolio-scorecard call sites, leaving those tables unstyled). Sidebar reorganized into workflow groups (deal execution / analysis and profitability / reference / operations). (2) DD workspace: migration `0039_project_checklist_item_state.sql` (FK → md_project — a `project(id)` reference would have been a dangling FK; the table is md_project) + `GET/POST /api/projects/:id/dd-workspace/{commercial|technical|legal}` merging gate-checklist YAML items (checklists/ SSOT, line-scanner parser in lib/library.ts) with per-item states and progress rollups; DdWorkspaceTab renders per-kind progress, mandatory-open list, and per-item pass/fail/na/waived assessment (waiver_id validated against the project's waivers; audited). (3) Profitability: `GET /api/profitability` (portfolio rollup of latest persisted NPV quantiles) + `GET /api/projects/:id/profitability` (simulation history) + /profitability page reusing SimulationDistChart. (4) Persistence: migration `0040_report_snapshot.sql` + `POST/GET /api/projects/:id/snapshots` assembling the current runs/simulations/RAG/DD-progress/reviews into an immutable markdown snapshot (the `document` table is a content-less registry — previously nothing let a user persist analysis state) + `GET /api/projects/:id/activity` timeline (new activity-and-reports tab). (5) Benchmarks: migration `0041_benchmark_case.sql` + `GET /api/benchmarks` (search/filter, all roles) + admin CRUD + idempotent seed of 8 public deals (scripts/co-newbiz/seed-benchmark-cases.ts) + /benchmarks card UI. (6) Admin: POST /api/admin/users (create), account delete + reset_password actions (sessions/tokens revoked, audited), master-data management (`GET/POST/PUT /api/admin/master-data` with allow-listed md_* tables/columns; staging_unmapped moved to /api/admin/master-data/staging with map/reject resolution), new MasterDataSection. New `/library` page (three-tab master-detail, gate badges in Korean, search, minimal markdown renderer with frontmatter stripping) + AppShell nav entry (regulations and checklists). Admin console's stale "no API yet" comment block corrected (governance/master-data/adr endpoints have existed since 2026-08-27). **Operational fix**: `db/client.ts` imports `bun:sqlite`, so `next start` under plain Node 500'd on every DB route — `dev`/`start` scripts now pin the Bun runtime (`bun --bun next ...`) and package.json description documents why. - **[2026-08-29]**: feat(skills): **Lifecycle phase system (0–7) wired into skill graph.** New `docs/phase-definitions.md` (standard workspace structure) maps co-newbiz's six stage gates one-to-one onto numbered phases: 0 intake, 1 Screening, 2 Pre-FS, 3 FS, 4 DD, 5 Entity/Group IC, 6 execution & post-close, 7 lifecycle finalization & QA — a deliberate, recorded deviation from the standard 0–6 table so the human IC approval boundary stays a distinct phase. All 42 `variant.json` `skill_manifest.variant_specific[]` entries gained `phases:` arrays, derived mechanically from the procedure schemas' `gate:` fields (not hand-assigned). `scripts/generate-skill-graph.ts` gained Source 3b: the original manifest loop only iterates workspace-root `templates/co-*`, so a scaffolded project's own `variant.json` never produced phase/used_by edges (every existing project graph, including co-consult's, had zero `phase` edges); the new branch reads the project-local `variant.json`. ### Added
- **[2026-08-28]**: feat(web): **3-stage review pipeline and human-final gate approval implemented — the two review-persistence gaps flagged in the prior design-vs-actual audit are closed.** `analysis_task`, `review_stage`, and the new `review_audit_sample` (migration `0038_review_audit_sample.sql` — the "AI review reliability" governance KPI in design §6.6 had no measurement table at all until now) all gain real write/read paths: `GET/POST /api/projects/:id/analysis-tasks` (GET lazy-creates the `ic_memo`/`rag_assessment` required-deliverable rows the first time a project reaches `entity_ic`/`group_ic`) + `POST .../analysis-tasks/:taskId/review` (the sole write path for `reviewed_by` — sets it to the calling analyst/admin, refuses a second sign-off, viewer-role callers get 403); `GET/POST /api/projects/:id/review-stages` (records AI 1st/2nd/human-final stages; `unverified_items` is a required array, never defaulted, per the design's no-PASS-stamp rule; an `ai_2nd` stage sharing its `reviewer_domain` with the run's `ai_1st` stage is refused with `same_domain_review`, enforcing design §5.7's cross-domain rule mechanically instead of leaving it as an unenforced schema field); `GET/POST /api/projects/:id/review-audit-samples` (samples an `ai_2nd` conclusion's agreement with `human_final`, aggregates `{total, agreed, agreed_pct}`). New "Review Status" tab (`ReviewStatusTab.tsx`, wired into the project detail page) renders all three: pending-vs-signed-off deliverables with a sign-off button, the 3-stage timeline per run (with its declared unverified-items list, even when empty), and the reliability KPI. `skills/agent-output-provenance/SKILL.md` (1.2.0) and the design-doc addendum both updated to record the gaps as closed rather than open. New tests: 4 cases covering lazy-creation/no-duplication, sign-off + double-sign-off refusal, viewer-403, unverified-items-omission refusal, and the full ai_1st→same-domain-refused→ai_2nd→human_final→audit-sample flow. `docs/openapi.yaml` gained the 6 new route entries (drift gate). Full suite 936/936 pass; `bun run build` clean; `bun scripts/audit.ts` clean.

Separately confirmed (not a bug, verified via ADR-0013/0043 + `agents/README.md`'s "4 classes" contract + `scripts/agent-create.ts`'s hardcoded flat root) that the `agents/` directory being flat rather than the design's `_core/domains/_shared` physical tree was a deliberate, documented substitution (YAML `domain:`/`subdomain:` frontmatter carries the grouping instead), not an oversight. Two genuine gaps confirmed by direct code inspection and flagged in a new design-doc addendum (§20) and in `skills/agent-output-provenance/SKILL.md` (1.1.0, corrected: the `analysis_task` table DOES exist since migration 0011 and is already read by the kill-criteria engine, but no code path ever writes a row into it, so the "unreviewed count" kill-criteria check is currently vacuously true rather than a real gate): (1) the 3-stage-review persistence layer (`review_stage` table, `reviewer_domain` column) is schema-only with no repository/API/UI consumer anywhere; (2) human-final gate approval (`analysis_task.reviewed_by`) has never been exercised on any of the running pilots (002-004, 011-014) — axis C's "real deal review" claim currently means AI 1st/2nd-stage review only. Full suite 932/932 pass (dependency removal verified via `bun install` + `bun test`); `bun scripts/audit.ts` clean.
- **[2026-08-28]**: feat(web): **ROIC projection v1 (ADR-0018 base-rate anchoring) — the last engine-pending Model-tab item now has a real, honest implementation.** `GET /api/projects/:id/roic-tree` now derives a per-fiscal-year historical ROIC series from `fact_industry` (previously only the latest year was used) and a 3-year-forward CAGR projection when at least two historical data points exist — under two points it stays explicitly `computable: false` with a reason, never fabricating a trend from one point. Each computed projection carries an `anchor_ref` back to the historical series it was derived from (ADR-0018's anchoring requirement) and flags `base_rate_warning` per year when the projected value lands above the historical max by more than 10% of the historical range (ADR-0018's mechanical outlier check — peer-distribution comparison is not wired yet, so v1 is historical-only). `RoicTree.tsx`'s `EnginePendingPanel` placeholder for projection is replaced with a real table (fiscal year / projected ROIC / base-rate flag) plus the anchor_ref and CAGR. Also fixed a stray stray Chinese-character typo (corrected to the Korean wording) found in the adjacent, previously-uncommitted customer-attrition CoC section (`MaReviewTab.tsx`, ADR-0072/migration 0037/`contract-coc-clauses` route — implemented in a prior session, now completed and synced) and added its missing `docs/openapi.yaml` entries (caught by the drift gate). New tests: 5 cases across `contract-coc-clauses` (list/create/audit/invalid-direction) and `roic-tree` projection (CAGR + base-rate-warning trip, and the <2-datapoints non-computable path). Full suite 932/932 pass; `bun run build` clean.
- **[2026-08-28]**: feat(sim): **Real Monte Carlo distribution — the engine's per-iteration draws are now persisted and served as an aggregate.** `python/monte_carlo.py` gained an opt-in `include_draws` scenario flag (draws output 4dp-rounded; quantiles still computed on unrounded draws, so the pinned seeded-reproduction values do not move — simulate/mcp/cli-parity tests unchanged). `db/lib/simulation.ts` 1.1.0 requests the draws, validates length/finiteness before anything persists (a truncated array aborts the whole write), and stores one `simulation_draw` row per iteration in the same transaction as the summary — the migration 0005 retention contract is unchanged (draws purgeable after N months, quantiles permanent). `GET /api/simulations/:id/results` now serves a server-side 40-bin histogram + 100-point CDF (`distribution`), while raw draw ROWS stay API-never-exposed (the existing `draws`-absence contract is still pinned); `SimulationDistChart.tsx` renders the real distribution (labelled as measured, with draw count and bin count) and keeps the illustrative quantile reconstruction only as the pre-draw fallback. Tests: two new pins — the degenerate no-liability fixture (single-spike distribution, honestly asserted) and a synthetic 100-draw spread simulation verifying exact bin counts and CDF endpoints without an engine spawn. Full suite 928/928 pass; build clean.

- **[2026-08-26]**: feat(web-stack): **Post-migration follow-through — ADR-0069/0070 formally Accepted; broken test suite fixed; Tailwind wired.** (1) ADR-0069/0070 status changed Proposed → Accepted (project owner sign-off, retroactive to the already-executed and verified Phases 0-6); ADR-0070 explicitly records its lint-enforcement gap as accepted follow-up debt, not silently dropped. (2) **Fixed a real regression**: `tests/unit/api.test.ts` (117 tests), `mcp.test.ts` (32 tests), and `openapi.test.ts` (4 tests) all imported `app/server/index.ts`, deleted in Phase 6 — the whole suite was broken. New `tests/unit/helpers/next-route-dispatch.ts` provides `nextRequest`/`nextFetch`, file-system-based dispatchers that resolve a path to the matching `app/web-next/app/api/**/route.ts` handler (mirroring Next.js's own route-matching precedence), used as drop-in replacements for Hono's `app.request()` — all 117+32 test bodies needed zero changes beyond the setup section. `openapi.test.ts`'s route-declaration scanner was rewritten for Next.js's exported-function convention instead of Hono's method-chain calls. Found and fixed two secondary bugs during this port: `lib/glossary.ts` resolved its directory via `process.cwd()` (correct only under `next dev`, broken under `bun test`) — switched to the same `import.meta.dirname` + cwd-walk-up-fallback pattern as ADR-0071's `proc-resolve.ts` fix; and one test asserted Hono's 404-for-unmatched-verb convention where Next.js correctly returns 405. Full suite: 926/926 passing. (3) **Tailwind CSS wired** (`tailwindcss` + `@tailwindcss/postcss`, `postcss.config.mjs`) — `app/web-next/app/styles.css` restructured into `@theme`-adjacent `:root`/`@media` tokens plus `@layer base`/`@layer components`, every existing rule preserved property-for-property (zero utility-class rewrites, since no component references Tailwind utilities yet and visual QA was not possible this session — login is off-limits per policy). Verified via computed-style inspection in a live browser: pixel-identical output pre/post conversion.

- **[2026-08-23]**: Kill-criteria evaluation record (ADR-0058 §1) + evidence_ref citation vocabulary (ADR-0058 §2) (task #56, user-approved 2026-08-23) — the two follow-ups ADR-0057 left open. **(1) Migration `0023_kill_criteria_evaluation.sql` + `db/repositories/kill-criteria.repository.ts` (1.0.0)** — the durable record the read-only evaluator deferred: a NEW run-scoped append-only `kill_criteria_evaluation` table (deliberately NOT `compliance_check` — project-scoped, its `rule_key` namespace feeds the six-validator newest-row-wins machinery — and NOT `gate_pass_record`, which is a procedure output_type, not a table; the future gate-flow step READS this table). **Complete ledger**: one row per evaluated criterion in ALL three outcomes (`triggered`/`false`/`unassessed`) — a recorded `false` is auditable proof of evaluation; row absence means never-evaluated, the only silent state. Each row snapshots `predicate_json`/`action`/`policy_id`/`thresholds_source` (ADR-0005 — a later registry edit cannot rewrite what a run was evaluated under); `UNIQUE (run_id, procedure_key, criterion_id)`; second record throws. `recordKillCriteriaEvaluation` guards run-exists/same-project/no-prior-record/actor-nonempty/FR-SEC-08 membership/FR-SEC-06 insider BEFORE the transaction, then writes all rows + one `data_access_log` + one `audit_log` row atomically (`kill_criteria_evaluated` with the four counts, `:HALT-PATH` appended when triggered); an empty procedure records zero rows and still audits. **(2) `db/lib/kill-criteria.ts` 1.0.0→1.1.0** — `KillCriteriaResult.outcomes` carries the complete per-criterion ledger with both governance anchors (non-null by construction — load-time anti-tamper threw otherwise), which the recorder persists one row per entry. **(3) `db/lib/evidence-ref.ts` (1.0.0) + `dimension-input.repository.ts` 1.0.0→1.1.0** — the v1.1 hardening of the 2026-08-22 design's Decision 2 flagged debt: `evidence_ref` on `requires_evidence_ref=1` vars must now be in the citation vocabulary `doc:<document-id>` | `src:<md_source.code>` | `https://<url>` (no whitespace, no plain http — a free-text citation like `cim.pdf#p12` names evidence only its author can find again). No `document`-table FK (content-addressed stub, zero readers — an FK to an unused table would be decoration, the ADR-0046 failure mode); snapshot-filler computed provenance and ungoverned tables (`value_chain_link`) explicitly out of scope; flag=0 vars keep format-free refs. **Tests**: 790→818 pass (+28) — `migration-0023.test.ts` (shape/CHECK matrices/UNIQUE triple/FKs/recency index/idempotence), recorder describes in `kill-criteria.test.ts` (complete ledger + audit rows, false-recorded, second-record throw, read-only re-evaluate, empty-procedure audit, non-member/MNPI/unknown-run/cross-project/empty-actor guards), `evidence-ref.test.ts` pure matrix, `dimension-input-write.test.ts` vocabulary gate tests + fixture updates to vocabulary shapes. **Docs**: ADR-0058 (Accepted) + README row; `docs/db-schema.md` (0023 status sentence, `kill_criteria_evaluation` row, `dimension_input` vocabulary note); `procedures/_kill-criteria/README.md` runtime section now records the recorder; `docs/rag-framework.md` §5-4 open-decision sentence resolved. - **[2026-08-23]**: Kill-criteria evaluator + first registry activation + ADR status hygiene (task #55, user-approved 2026-08-23) — the follow-up PR #90's staging explicitly reserved. **(1) NEW `db/lib/kill-criteria.ts` (1.0.0, ADR-0057)** — the runtime consumer the registry waited on: `evaluateKillCriteria(db, {runId, projectId, procedure, industryIds?, now?})` loads the procedure's `procedures/_kill-criteria/<procedure>__*.json` entries, validates every one at load time (the registry check-(c) anti-tamper gate re-enforced at runtime — enabled-without-`policy_id`+`thresholds_source` **throws**; action outside the `halt`/`mark_conditional`/`escalate_to_risk_officer` vocabulary **throws**; unparseable predicate **throws** — violations are surfaced, never silently skipped), then evaluates the **enabled, predicate-carrying** entries against the run's `project_variable_snapshot` through the SAME ADR-0046 predicate engine `rule-assessment.ts` uses (`parsePredicate`/`evaluatePredicate`, Kleene semantics, identical var coercion). NEW `db/repositories/classification.repository.ts`: the single write path for `project_case_classification` enforcing **Decision-5a asymmetry** — `origin='external'` (validator-set-widening) single-writer with mandatory rationale; `origin ∈ {internal, hybrid, joint}` (reducing) additionally requires the approver trio `approver_role` (closed authority list: process-governance-manager / pm / group_ic, each sourced from governance-operations.md §2/§5 waiver-authority and change-plan rows — an ordered list, NOT the free text `issueWaiver()` accepts; comment mandates an ADR before the roster grows) + `approved_by` (an active member DISTINCT from classified_by, insider-checked on MNPI projects) + `approved_at`; missing any one → named error with zero partial state (all checks run before the transaction opens). **Downgrade protection**: superseding a live external row with a reducing origin needs the full trio on the NEW row — the validator set narrows at supersession, so that is where the control fires. `supersede()` follows the single legal statement sequence 0013 documents (successor born non-live → predecessor retires → successor goes live; the reverse trips the partial unique index — a schema fact, not a convention), one transaction, full audit + write-log; `classify()` onto a live row and `supersede()` without one both rejected (append-only history, Decision 5b). NEW `db/repositories/deal-structure-hypothesis.repository.ts`: `hypothesize()` always births `four_axis_review_completed=0`; `completeFourAxisReview()` is the ONLY 0→1 setter (no flag field on the input type, pinned by @ts-expect-error test; artifact_ref to the PR-10 brownfield-fs four-axis output mandatory, recorded in the audit trail) — no naked flag writes exist in the module surface (pinned to exactly 3 exports). `db/lib/access-control.ts` 1.0.0 → 1.1.0: `requireActiveMember` (FR-SEC-08 write-path form), `requireInsiderIfMnpi` (FR-SEC-06 — live insider list checked at WRITE time, grant time is never proof), and **`setMnpiFlag`** — the only mnpi_flag write path, closing PR-4's surveyed gap: a 0→1 flip retroactively enrolls every then-active project_member onto insider_list in the SAME transaction (enrollment date = flip date; ON CONFLICT only clears `removed_at`, never rewrites `added_at` — a closed row re-activates with its original enrollment date, and later flips cannot launder provenance); 1→0 deliberately leaves the roster intact (it is the record of who HELD access — erasing it on downgrade would delete the trail an incident review needs); non-empty reason + active membership + existing project_classification row required, audited and logged. Every accepted mutation across all three repositories writes `logDataAccess(action:'write')` + `appendAudit` in-transaction; rejected writes log nothing. - **[2026-08-22]**: Stage 0 (design-first, ADR-0032) of the RAG-formalization and case-classification redesign — documentation only, no code. This closes a 6-perspective design review (risk-officer, process-governance-manager+compliance-officer, data-architect+platform-lead, market-intelligence-agent+quant-methodologist, brownfield-agent+greenfield-agent, legal-counsel) that overturned the original implementation plan on 7 points, most critically that the proposed classification-aware six-validator completeness check was a waiver bypass with none of a waiver's controls (approver/expiry/history/revocation) — 4 independent reviewers converged on this finding separately. `docs/designs/2026-08-22-rag-formalization-and-case-classification-design.md` (new `docs/designs/` dir, 477 lines) records the full revised design: a typed JSON predicate AST with Kleene three-valued logic (not a DSL, not decomposed rows), a `project_variable_snapshot`-based evidence layer (replacing a dynamic per-var query design found to have SQL-injection surface and no ordering column on half its target tables), a 4-value `origin` axis (`internal`/`external`/`hybrid`/`joint` — the 3-value original had no room for a true bilateral JV), asymmetric classification write authority (single-party for `origin=external`, two-party approval for the three values that reduce the validator set), and a `not_applicable` compliance-check result so a classification-driven skip is recorded honestly instead of as a false `pass`. `docs/rag-framework.md` gains a Market-dimension staged reduced-check subsection and a corrected evidence mapping (`concentration_hhi` measures bargaining power, not competitive intensity — the original mapping could silently misgrade in either direction). Five ADRs drafted at Proposed status (`0046` predicate AST + evidence registry, `0047` three-axis classification model + `case_subtype` retirement + mandatory `project_policy_pin` backfill for in-flight projects, `0048` amends ADR-0037 to scope the six-validator no-waiver rule to applicable validators, `0049` amends ADR-0016 to add UNASSESSED as a fourth grade state and a Green-cap on uncalibrated market/technical/risk rules, `0050` records ADR-0045's dangling reference as an errata note rather than rewriting an Accepted decision). Three open-decision agendas (`docs/decisions/open/{market,technical-execution,risk}-thresholds.md`, 51 TBD markers, zero invented numbers) stage the future threshold-setting meeting. Formal transcript: `memory/meeting-2026-08-22-rag-formalization-review.md`. Full suite **278 pass / 0 fail** (unchanged — documentation-only change). `bun scripts/audit.ts` all checks passed.

- **[2026-08-20]**: Stage 5 Wave 5.10 (system pilot — re-run a Stage 3 manual pilot through the built system, compare results, per `docs/dev-plan.md`'s acceptance rule: "a discrepancy is investigated as a defect in one or the other, never left unexplained") — `docs/pilots/pilot-001-system-rerun.md` re-runs pilot 001 (HD Hyundai / STX Heavy Industries) against the real built system: `bun scripts/co-newbiz/newbiz.ts proc:resolve --procedure brownfield-screening` (matched the manual pilot's 3-step walkthrough exactly) and `bun scripts/co-newbiz/newbiz.ts rag` with only the one dimension the manual pilot had real public data for (Regulatory/Permit, behavioral remedy — matched the manual pilot's Amber grade and rationale exactly). **A real defect was found and documented, not silently absorbed**: the manual pilot explicitly declined to produce an overall RAG grade ("since not all six dimensions are gradable, no overall grade is produced — correctly, per the framework's own rule"), but the system re-run produced and persisted an overall grade (Amber) anyway — `db/lib/rag-engine.ts`'s `assessRun()` (Wave 5.3) defaults every dimension missing from the input scenario to Amber rather than leaving it genuinely unassessed, and always computes/writes an `overall` row from the resulting six (real-or-defaulted) grades. This contradicts the design's own stated rule (`docs/rag-framework.md`, `docs/ui-spec.md` §3, `skills/rag-scoring-framework/SKILL.md`'s own description) and traces to a real cross-wave gap: the engine (Wave 5.3) was built for a single-call, fully-specified-scenario use case before the web UI (Wave 5.6B) and the RAG-scoring skill (Wave 5.8I) later encoded the incremental, possibly-still-incomplete assessment model the design actually calls for — no prior test exercised the engine with a genuinely partial real-world input, only a fully-specified scenario or the API's empty-state (zero rows), never the CLI's actual default-fill behavior. **Tracked as a follow-up defect, not fixed in this PR** — the pilot re-run's own report documents the recommended fix direction (an explicit per-dimension `assessed` signal in the scenario contract, and skipping the `overall` write entirely rather than defaulting it, until all six dimensions have a real assessment on record) for a dedicated follow-up change, since it touches the engine's core evaluation function and its existing test suite. Full suite **181 pass / 0 fail** (no code change in this PR — pilot re-run plus documentation only). **This completes Stage 5's dev-plan.md wave table (5.1 through 5.10)** — the one open item is the rag-engine defect this pilot surfaced, tracked explicitly rather than left unaddressed.

- **[2026-08-20]**: Stage 5 Wave 5.9A (backup/restore/rollback infrastructure — first Wave 5.9 sub-PR, `docs/dev-plan.md`'s "one real rollback rehearsed on a reversible-tagged migration; one real recovery drill executed and logged" acceptance criterion) — `scripts/co-newbiz/backup.ts` (checkpointed SQLite copy + row-count manifest, ADR-0014/0015) and `scripts/co-newbiz/restore.ts` (restore + manifest-verified row-count check, refusing to restore a backup with no manifest). Every existing migration (0001-0009) retroactively tagged `@reversible: true` per ADR-0014's "every migration tagged reversible-or-not at write time" requirement — the actual reversibility mechanism in this codebase is pre-migration backup restore, not a per-migration DOWN script (SQLite + this project's forward-only migration runner). `db/migrations/0010_audit_log_created_at_index.sql` — a real, useful index on `audit_log(created_at DESC, id DESC)` for the admin audit-log viewer's unfiltered chronological scan (`query-performance-tuning`'s own flagged high-traffic pattern), doubling as the migration used to actually rehearse the rollback procedure rather than a synthetic throwaway. **A real defect was found and fixed during the rehearsal itself, not by code review**: the first restore attempt reported "verified" but the migration's effect was still present — `restore.ts` copied the backup's main file over the live path but left the live DB's `-wal` sidecar in place, and SQLite replayed those leftover post-backup WAL frames on next open, silently re-applying exactly the write the restore was meant to undo (the row-count check even passed, because it ran after the replay had already happened). Fixed by checkpoint-truncating the live connection's own WAL in place before closing and copying — re-rehearsed after the fix and confirmed correct. Both drills (recovery: seed → backup → delete → restore → verify content, not just counts; rollback: migrate through 0009 → backup → apply 0010 → confirm index present → restore → confirm index absent and `schema_migrations` back to 9) executed for real against scratch databases and logged in the new `docs/runbooks/backup-restore.md`. **6 tests in `tests/unit/backup-restore.test.ts`**, verify-then-encode (the manual CLI rehearsal ran first, including the failed first attempt, before any assertion was written). `scripts/SCRIPTS.md` gained 2 registry rows. Full suite **181 pass / 0 fail**. Remaining Wave 5.9 scope: broader deployment/runbook documentation and the rest of `docs/security-baseline.md`'s pre-deployment gate execution, tracked as a follow-on sub-PR.
